---
title: 15 stripes tartan setts (page 2)
date: 2023-03-18
weight: 15
---
509 setts across 458 tartans with 15 stripes, grouped by colour-sequence pattern. Patterns with fewer than 40 setts are listed in full below; busier ones link to their own page. Each variant shows ΔT — its mean distance (OKLab ΔE) from the pattern's base colours — nearest first, with a stripe bar in the nearest human-palette colour names (hover a stripe to read it).
